namespace = VIETmisc

VIETmisc.0002 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0002.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0002.desc
	theme = VIET_theme_oblivion_pastoral_dream
	
	right_portrait = root	

	trigger = {
		NOR = {
			has_character_modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_pells_gate
			has_character_modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_odiil_farm
			has_character_modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_weye
			has_character_modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_faregyl
			has_character_modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_cloud_ruler_temple
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0002.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_pells_gate
			days = 1095
		}		
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0002.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_odiil_farm
			days = 1095
		}		
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0002.c
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_weye
			days = 1095
		}		
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0002.d
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_faregyl
			days = 1095
		}		
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0002.e
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_oblivion_dream_cloud_ruler_temple
			days = 1095
		}		
	}
}

VIETmisc.0004 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0004.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0004.desc
	theme = friendly
	override_background = { reference = corridor_day }
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = boredom
	}
	

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_game_rule = VIET_serious_events }
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = honest
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0004.a
		add_stress = -5
		stress_impact = {
			shy = minor_stress_impact_loss
			gregarious = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}	
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0004.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_overheard_oblivion_conversation
			days = 365
		}
		stress_impact = {
			shy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			gregarious = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
	}
}

			add = 0.2

		}

		name = VIETmisc.0013.a

		}

	option = {

			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain

		}
	}

VIETmisc.0016 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0016.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0016.desc
	theme = VIET_theme_office
	
	right_portrait = root
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0016.a
		
		
		
		VIET_small_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_loss
			lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			diligent = minor_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}		
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0016.b
		VIET_small_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			calm = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}		
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0016.c
		trigger = {
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		trait = lazy
		VIET_medium_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			lazy = medium_stress_impact_loss
		}		
	}
}

		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0026.a
		VIET_small_prestige_loss_effect = yes

			50 = {
				desc = VIETmisc.0026.a.a
				send_interface_toast = {
					left_icon = ROOT
					title = VIETmisc.0026.toast.a
					add_stress = -2
				}
			}
			
			50 = {
				desc = VIETmisc.0026.a.b

					left_icon = ROOT
					title = VIETmisc.0026.toast.b
					add_stress = 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0033 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0033.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0033.desc
	theme = learning
	
	left_portrait = { 	
		character = root
		animation = personality_cynical
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = fickle
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0033.a
		stress_impact = {
			base = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			stubborn = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0033.b
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			stubborn = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		random_list = {
			70 = {
				desc = VIETmisc.0033.b.a
				send_interface_toast = {
					
					left_icon = ROOT
					title = VIETmisc.0033.toast.a
					custom_tooltip = VIETmisc.0033.toast.failure
				}			
			}
			30 = {
				desc = VIETmisc.0033.b.b
				send_interface_toast = {
					
					left_icon = ROOT
					title = VIETmisc.0033.toast.b
					custom_tooltip = VIETmisc.0033.toast.success
					random_list = {
						10 = { add_diplomacy_skill = 1 }
						10 = { add_martial_skill = 1 }
						10 = { add_stewardship_skill = 1 }
						10 = { add_intrigue_skill = 1 }
						10 = { add_learning_skill = 1 }					
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0041 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0041.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0041.desc
	theme = unfriendly
	override_background = { reference = garden }
	
	left_portrait = { 	
		character = scope:scope_VIET_slip_bumper
		animation = fear
	}	
	right_portrait = { 	
		character = root
		animation = anger
	}
	

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_game_rule = VIET_serious_events }
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_slip_bumper
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0041.a
		scope:scope_VIET_slip_bumper = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_didnt_get_angry
			}		
		}
		VIET_small_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			vengeful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0041.b
		scope:scope_VIET_slip_bumper = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_angry_at_me
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_bumped_into_me
			}			
		}
		add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		stress_impact = {
			forgiving = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			calm = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			humble = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0047 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0047.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0047.desc
	theme = learning
	override_background = { reference = sitting_room }
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_rational
	}
	

	trigger = {
		learning >= medium_skill_rating
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0047.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = diligent
					has_trait = ambitious
				}
			}
			stress_impact = {
				diligent = minor_stress_impact_gain
				ambitious = minor_stress_impact_gain
			}
		}
		else = {
			stress_impact = {
				base = minor_stress_impact_loss
				shy = major_stress_impact_loss
			}		
		}
		VIET_medium_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_curled_up_with_good_book
			years = 5
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0047.b
		stress_impact = {
			base = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			shy = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
		}		
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_curled_up_with_good_book
			years = 1
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0048 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0048.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0048.desc
	theme = party
	override_background = { reference = market }
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = disapproval
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lustful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = chaste
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0048.a
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0048.b
		trigger = {
			has_trait = generous
		}
		trait = generous
		remove_short_term_gold = 5
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					trait_is_virtue = generous
				}
			}
			VIET_small_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			generous = minor_stress_impact_loss
			wrathful = minor_stress_impact_gain
			cynical = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0048.c
		trigger = {
			has_trait = sadistic
		}
		trait = sadistic
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_virtue = compassionate					
						trait_is_virtue = patient					
						trait_is_virtue = calm					
						trait_is_virtue = forgiving					
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		add_dread = 5
		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0048.d
		trigger = {
			diplomacy >= decent_skill_rating			
		}
		skill = diplomacy
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_constructive_criticism
			days = 730
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = { 
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						NOT = { has_sexuality = homosexual }
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						OR = {
							has_sexuality = homosexual						
							has_sexuality = bisexual						
						}
					}
				}
			}
			text = VIETmisc.0048.e
		}
		name = { 
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						NOT = { has_sexuality = homosexual }
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						OR = {
							has_sexuality = homosexual						
							has_sexuality = bisexual						
						}
					}
				}
			}
			text = VIETmisc.0048.f
		}
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_trait = celibate
				has_sexuality = asexual
			}			
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_lustful_ruler
			days = 365
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0061 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0061.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0061.desc
	theme = secret
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_callous
	}	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner
		animation = fear
	}
	
	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			num_sinful_traits >= 1
			num_virtuous_traits <= 1
		}	
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = honest
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = zealous
		}
	}	
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				num_sinful_traits >= 1
				num_virtuous_traits <= 1
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_haunted_sinner
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0061.a
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			cynical = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_haunted_by_ghost_for_sin
				years = 3
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_prayed_for_me
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_friend_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_friend_reason_placeholder
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0061.b
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			humble = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			zealous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_haunted_by_ghost_for_sin
				years = 3
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_nagged_me
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_friend_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_friend_reason_placeholder
		}
	}
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0061.c
		add_dread = 10
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		VIET_small_piety_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_haunted_by_ghost_for_sin
				years = 6
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_doesnt_care_about_me
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_rival_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_rival_reason_placeholder
		}
	}
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0061.d
		add_dread = 10
		VIET_small_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			cynical = minor_stress_impact_loss
			trusting = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_haunted_by_ghost_for_sin
				years = 6
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_doesnt_care_about_me
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_rival_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_haunted_sinner
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_rival_reason_placeholder
		}
	}
}

		animation = disapproval
	}

		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
		}	
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			has_trait = cynical
		}	
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_sandman_dreamer
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				has_trait = cynical
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_sandman_skeptic
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0095.a
		scope:scope_VIET_sandman_dreamer = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_believed_sandman_story
			}		
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_sandman_skeptic = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_sandman_dialogue_disagree
			}		
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0095.b
		scope:scope_VIET_sandman_dreamer = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_didnt_believe_sandman_story
			}		
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_sandman_skeptic = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_sandman_dialogue_agree
			}		
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0107 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0107.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0107.desc
	theme = vassal
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:scope_VIET_court_sleepy
		animation = pain
	}
	
	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NAND = {
				has_trait = zealous
				religion = religion:islam_religion
			}
			OR = {
				has_trait = gregarious
				has_trait = drunkard
				has_trait = lustful
				has_trait = lazy
				has_trait = trusting
				has_trait = gluttonous
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gluttonous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = temperate
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NAND = {
					has_trait = zealous
					religion = religion:islam_religion
				}
				OR = {
					has_trait = gregarious
					has_trait = drunkard
					has_trait = lustful
					has_trait = lazy
					has_trait = trusting
					has_trait = gluttonous
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_court_sleepy
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_bad_hangover
				years = 3
			}
		}	
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0107.a
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		scope:scope_VIET_court_sleepy = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_bad_hangover_break
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_friend_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_court_sleepy
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_friend_reason_placeholder
		}
	}
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0107.b
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		scope:scope_VIET_court_sleepy = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_bad_hangover_relief
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_friend_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_court_sleepy
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_friend_reason_placeholder
		}		
	}
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0107.c
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_amused_ruler
			days = 777
		}
		stress_impact = {
			base = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_court_sleepy = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_bad_hangover_mockery
			}		
		}		
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = sadistic
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0132.a
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_trait = lunatic_genetic
				has_trait = lunatic_1
			}
		}
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			impatient = minor_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = minor_stress_impact_gain
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}		
	}		
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0132.b
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_trait = lunatic_genetic
				has_trait = lunatic_1
			}
		}
		add_dread = 5
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			patient = minor_stress_impact_gain
			forgiving = minor_stress_impact_gain
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
			calm = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}			
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0132.c
		trigger = {
			has_trait = lunatic_1
			NOT = {	has_trait = lunatic_genetic	}
		}
		trait = lunatic_1
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_hears_voices_like_thamriel
			years = 5
		}
	}				
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0132.c
		trigger = {
			has_trait = lunatic_genetic
			NOT = {	has_trait = lunatic_1 }
		}
		trait = lunatic_genetic
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_hears_voices_like_thamriel
			years = 5
		}
	}	
}

VIETmisc.0135 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0135.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0135.desc
	theme = unfriendly
	
	left_portrait = { 	
		character = scope:scope_VIET_sad_person
		animation = sadness
	}	
	right_portrait = root	

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = fickle
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_sad_person
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0135.a
		VIET_small_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		scope:scope_VIET_sad_person = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_comforted_me
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_friend_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_sad_person
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_friend_reason_placeholder
		}
		stress_impact = {
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}		
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0135.b
	}			
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0135.c
		VIET_small_piety_loss_effect = yes
		scope:scope_VIET_sad_person = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_mocked_me
			}		
		}
		progress_towards_rival_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:scope_VIET_sad_person
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = VIET_rival_reason_placeholder
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			humble = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}

VIETmisc.0139 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0139.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0139.desc
	theme = vassal
	
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:scope_VIET_beggar_friend

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			has_trait_benevolent_trigger = yes 
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = honest
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				has_trait_benevolent_trigger = yes 
			}
			save_scope_as = scope_VIET_beggar_friend
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_beggar_friend
				years = 10
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0139.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					trait_is_virtue = generous
				}
			}
			VIET_large_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0139.b
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			humble = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:scope_VIET_beggar_friend = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_judgmental
			}		
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0139.c
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				gold >= 10
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = 10
		VIET_huge_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_hired_beggar_servant
			years = 10
		}
		stress_impact = {
			arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			paranoid = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
}

			}

			random = {
				chance = 50

					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					left_icon = scope:scope_VIET_tripper

					scope:scope_VIET_tripper = {
						increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = fall }
					}
				}
			}		
		}
	}

VIETmisc.0157 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0157.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0157.desc
	theme = feast_activity
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = disgust
	}	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:horny
		animation = schadenfreude
	}
	
	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			is_married = no
			is_female = no
			NOR = {
				has_sexuality = asexual
				has_sexuality = homosexual
				has_trait = chaste
				has_trait = celibate
				is_eunuch_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				has_trait = lustful
				has_trait = gregarious
				has_trait = arbitrary
				has_trait = lazy
				has_trait = gluttonous
			}
		}	
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lustful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = chaste
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gluttonous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = temperate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				is_married = no
				is_female = no
				NOR = {
					has_sexuality = asexual
					has_sexuality = homosexual
					has_trait = chaste
					has_trait = celibate
					is_eunuch_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					has_trait = lustful
					has_trait = gregarious
					has_trait = arbitrary
					has_trait = lazy
					has_trait = gluttonous
				}
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_100_love_techniques
				years = 10
			}
			save_scope_as = horny
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0157.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_sin = lustful
						trait_is_virtue = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_virtue = lustful
						trait_is_sin = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_loss_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			lustful = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0157.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_sin = lustful
						trait_is_virtue = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_virtue = lustful
						trait_is_sin = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_loss_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		VIET_large_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		scope:horny = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_love_techniques_prude
			}		
		}
		stress_impact = {
			lustful = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0157.c
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_sin = lustful
						trait_is_virtue = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_loss_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_virtue = lustful
						trait_is_sin = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_loss_effect = yes
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_100_love_techniques
			years = 10
		}
		scope:horny = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_love_techniques_agreement
			}		
		}
		stress_impact = {
			chaste = medium_stress_impact_gain
			celibate = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
}

	left_portrait = { 	
		character = scope:courtier
		animation = personality_rational
	}	
	right_portrait = root
	

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = callous
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = courtier
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0165.a

			lazy = minor_stress_impact_loss

VIETmisc.0175 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0175.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0175.desc
	theme = intrigue_temptation_focus
	override_background = { reference = council_chamber }
	
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = boredom
	}	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:horny
		animation = personality_vengeful
	}
	
	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait_extroverted_trigger = yes
				has_trait_malicious_trigger = yes
				has_trait = paranoid
				has_trait = arrogant
			}
			NOR = {
				has_trait_benevolent_trigger = yes
				has_trait = humble
			}	
			is_female = no
			is_married = no
			num_of_relation_lover < 1
			NOT = { has_sexuality = homosexual }
			NOT = { has_sexuality = asexual }
		}	
	}
	
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = chaste
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lustful
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait_extroverted_trigger = yes
					has_trait_malicious_trigger = yes
					has_trait = paranoid
					has_trait = arrogant
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait_benevolent_trigger = yes
					has_trait = humble
				}	
				is_female = no
				is_married = no
				num_of_relation_lover < 1
				NOT = { has_sexuality = homosexual }
				NOT = { has_sexuality = asexual }
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_obsessed_with_a_courtesan
				years = 6
			}
			save_scope_as = horny
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0175.a		
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_sin = chaste
						trait_is_virtue = lustful
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_medium_piety_loss_effect = yes
		}		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_sin = lustful
						trait_is_virtue = chaste
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {		
			cynical = miniscule_stress_impact_gain		
			lustful = minor_stress_impact_gain		
			gregarious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
		}	
		scope:horny = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shamed_courtesan
			}			
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0175.b
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {		
			paranoid = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
		}	
		scope:horny = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shamed_courtesan
			}			
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0175.c
		trigger = {
			can_add_hook = {
				type = threat_hook
				target = scope:horny
			}
		}
		add_hook = {
			type = threat_hook
			target = scope:horny
			years = 5
		}
		VIET_large_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {		
			content = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			craven = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
			lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
			forgiving = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
			trusting = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
		}	
		scope:horny = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_suspects_terrible_things
			}			
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0175.d
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_eunuch_trigger = yes
				has_trait = celibate
				has_sexuality = asexual
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					is_female = no
					NOT = { has_sexuality = homosexual }
				}
				AND = {
					is_female = yes
					OR = {
						has_sexuality = homosexual						
						has_sexuality = bisexual						
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_lustful_ruler
			years = 5
		}
		stress_impact = {		
			chaste = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}	
		scope:horny = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_wants_my_courtesan
			}			
		}
	}
}

			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_night_we_met
				years = 8
			}
			save_scope_as = sad_guy
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0178.a
		random_list = {
			10 = { VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes }
			10 = { VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes }
		}
		stress_impact = {		
			deceitful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			stubborn = miniscule_stress_impact_gain	
			impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:sad_guy = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_moving_on_advice
			}		
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0178.b
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {		
			cynical = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain

		}	
		scope:sad_guy = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_moving_on_advice
			}		
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0178.c
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes

VIETmisc.0180 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0180.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0180.desc
	theme = learning_scholarship_focus

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:bad_handwriting	
		animation = chancellor	
	}	
	right_portrait = { 	
		character = root
		animation = disapproval
	}	
	

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			
			NOT = { has_trait = blind }
			probably_unintelligent_trigger = no
			OR = {
				probably_intelligent_trigger = yes
				diplomacy >= decent_skill_rating
				learning >= decent_skill_rating
			}
		}
		
		NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_tribal }		
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				
				NOT = { has_trait = blind }
				probably_unintelligent_trigger = no
				OR = {
					probably_intelligent_trigger = yes
					diplomacy >= decent_skill_rating
					learning >= decent_skill_rating
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = bad_handwriting
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = VIET_horrible_handwriting }
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = VIET_horrible_handwriting
				}
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0180.a
		scope:bad_handwriting = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_mocked_handwriting
			}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			}
			stress_impact = {
				base = minor_stress_impact_loss
				sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
				arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = compassionate
			}
			stress_impact = {
				compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
				humble = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			}
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0180.b
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			target = scope:bad_handwriting
			10 = { 
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -1.1
				}
				desc = VIETmisc.0180.tease_toast_bad
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					left_icon = scope:bad_handwriting
					title = VIETmisc.0180.tease_toast_bad
					scope:bad_handwriting = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = root
							modifier = VIET_opinion_tease_handwriting_bad
						}
					}
				}
			}
			10 = { 
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 1.1
				}
				desc = VIETmisc.0180.tease_toast_good
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					left_icon = scope:bad_handwriting
					title = VIETmisc.0180.tease_toast_good
					scope:bad_handwriting = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = root
							modifier = VIET_opinion_tease_handwriting_good
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0180.c
		stress_impact = {
			gregarious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0180.d
		trigger = {
			learning >= decent_skill_rating			
		}
		skill = learning
		scope:bad_handwriting = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_constructive_criticism_handwriting
			}		
		}
		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		VIET_small_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		random = {
			chance = 30
			modifier = {
				add = 3
				has_trait = patient
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -3
				has_trait = impatient
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 3
				has_trait = calm
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -3
				has_trait = wrathful
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 3
				has_trait = diligent
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -3
				has_trait = lazy
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 4
				learning >= decent_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -4
				learning <= low_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 3
				learning >= medium_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -3
				learning <= mediocre_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 3
				stewardship >= decent_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -3
				stewardship <= low_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 2
				stewardship >= medium_skill_rating
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -2
				stewardship <= mediocre_skill_rating
			}
			custom_tooltip = VIETmisc.0180.tease_toast_great
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				left_icon = scope:bad_handwriting
				title = VIETmisc.0180.tease_toast_great
				scope:bad_handwriting = {
					remove_character_modifier = VIET_horrible_handwriting
				}
			}
		}
	}	
}

	}

		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = honest
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0235.a
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
			diligent = minor_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}	
	}	
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0235.b
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			humble = minor_stress_impact_gain
			content = minor_stress_impact_gain
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}	
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0235.c
		trigger = {
			has_trait = generous
		}
		trait = generous
		remove_short_term_gold = 10
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					trait_is_virtue = generous
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_charity_to_servant
			years = 4
		}
		stress_impact = {
			callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}	
	}

}

	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0239.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0239.desc

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:generalist
		animation = personality_rational
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:specialist
		animation = disapproval
	}

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = arrogant
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = ambitious
				has_trait = impatient
			}
		}
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOR = {
				has_trait = arrogant
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = ambitious
				has_trait = impatient
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = impatient
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = specialist
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOR = {
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = impatient
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = generalist
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0239.a
		scope:specialist = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shares_my_opinions
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shares_my_opinions
			}			
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0239.b
		scope:generalist = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shares_my_opinions
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shares_my_opinions
			}			
		}
	}
}

VIETmisc.0241 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0241.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0241.desc
	theme = seduction
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_rational
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0241.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_comforted_servant
			years = 4
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_virtue = compassionate					
						trait_is_virtue = just				
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_small_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
			arbitrary = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}	
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0241.b
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			humble = minor_stress_impact_gain
			just = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0241.c
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_heartless_towards_servants
			years = 4
		}
		add_dread = 8
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = callous
					has_trait = sadistic
					has_trait = honest
				}
				NOT = { has_trait = compassionate }
			}
			stress_impact = {
				callous = minor_stress_impact_loss
				sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
				honest = minor_stress_impact_loss
			}
		}
		else = {
			stress_impact = {
				compassionate = medium_stress_impact_gain
				humble = medium_stress_impact_gain
				just = minor_stress_impact_gain
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0241.d
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_defended_servants_honor
			years = 4
		}
		VIET_medium_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith = {
					OR = {
						trait_is_virtue = compassionate					
						trait_is_virtue = just				
					}
				}
			}
			VIET_huge_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			craven = minor_stress_impact_gain
			callous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = minor_stress_impact_gain
			calm = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
}

		animation = personality_compassionate
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:realist
		animation = disapproval
	}

	trigger = {
		any_courtier_or_guest = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = cynical
				has_trait = callous
				has_trait = sadistic
				has_trait = paranoid
				has_trait = depressed_1
				has_trait = depressed_genetic
			}
		}
		any_courtier_or_guest = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOR = {
				has_trait = cynical
				has_trait = callous
				has_trait = sadistic
				has_trait = paranoid
				has_trait = depressed_1
				has_trait = depressed_genetic
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = fickle
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = cynical
					has_trait = callous
					has_trait = sadistic
					has_trait = paranoid
					has_trait = depressed_1
					has_trait = depressed_genetic
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = realist
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOR = {
					has_trait = cynical
					has_trait = callous
					has_trait = sadistic
					has_trait = paranoid
					has_trait = depressed_1
					has_trait = depressed_genetic
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = optimist
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0243.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_positive_mindset
			years = 5
		}
		scope:optimist = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_approves_viewpoint
			}		
		}
		scope:realist = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_disapproves_viewpoint
			}		
		}
		stress_impact = {
			cynical = medium_stress_impact_gain
			paranoid = minor_stress_impact_gain
			depressed_1 = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			depressed_genetic = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0243.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_realist_mindset
			years = 5
		}
		scope:realist = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_approves_viewpoint
			}		
		}
		scope:optimist = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_disapproves_viewpoint
			}		
		}
		stress_impact = {
			trusting = minor_stress_impact_gain
			zealous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}

	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0247.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0247.desc
	theme = VIET_theme_garden_generic
	override_icon = { reference = "gfx/interface/icons/event_types/type_relation_friendly.dds" } 
	
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:praiser
		animation = personality_forgiving
	}

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOT = { has_trait = shy }
			opinion = {
				target = root
				value > -30
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOT = { has_trait = shy }
				opinion = {
					target = root
					value > -30
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = praiser
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0247.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_encouraged_by_others
			years = 5
		}
		scope:praiser = {
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_encouragement
			}		
		}
		VIET_medium_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			paranoid = minor_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0247.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_suspicious_of_praise
			years = 5
		}
		scope:praiser = {
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_suspects_ulterior_motives
			}		
		}
		VIET_medium_piety_loss_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			trusting = minor_stress_impact_gain
			humble = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0247.c
		scope:praiser = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_awkward_silence
			}		
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_awkward_silence
			}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = shy
			}
			stress_impact = {
				shy = minor_stress_impact_loss
			}
		}
		else = {
			stress_impact = {
				gregarious = minor_stress_impact_gain
				trusting = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
				callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			}
		}
	}

}

VIETmisc.0249 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0249.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0249.desc
	theme = stewardship_duty_focus
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:councilor
		animation = throne_room_conversation_2
	}
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = boredom
	}

	trigger = {
		
		
		
		
		
		
		any_councillor = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
		}	
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = zealous
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_councillor = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = councilor
		}	
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0249.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = diligent
				}
			}
			stress_impact = {
				diligent = minor_stress_impact_gain
				ambitious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			}
		}
		else = {
			stress_impact = {
				base = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
				lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			}
		}
		scope:councilor = {
			VIET_huge_prestige_loss_effect = yes
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				opinion = -10
				modifier = insulted_opinion
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0249.b
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}

}

VIETmisc.0251 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0251.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0251.desc
	theme = mental_break
	override_background = { reference = throne_room }
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:struggle_lover
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:struggle_hater
		animation = disapproval
	}

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = gluttonous
				has_trait = gregarious
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = arrogant
				has_trait = brave
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOR = {
				has_trait = gluttonous
				has_trait = gregarious
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = arrogant
				has_trait = brave
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = gluttonous
					has_trait = gregarious
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = brave
					has_trait = zealous
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = struggle_lover
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOR = {
					has_trait = gluttonous
					has_trait = gregarious
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = brave
					has_trait = zealous
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = struggle_hater
		}
		scope:struggle_hater = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:struggle_lover
				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_disagree
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = scope:struggle_lover
				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_disagree
			}			
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0251.a
		VIET_medium_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		scope:struggle_lover = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_agree
			}			
		}
		scope:struggle_hater = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_disagree
			}			
		}
		stress_impact = {
			craven = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0251.b
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		scope:struggle_lover = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_disagree
			}			
		}
		scope:struggle_hater = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_agree
			}			
		}
		stress_impact = {
			brave = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0251.c
	}

}

					has_trait = gregarious
					has_trait = brave
					has_trait = zealous

			}

			}

		}
	}

			}

			add = -0.2
			has_trait = fickle
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {

			has_trait = callous
		}
	}

	immediate = {

				OR = {
					has_trait = zealous

					has_trait = trusting
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = cynical
					has_trait = honest
					has_trait = content

				modifier = VIET_struggles_everywhere
				years = 10
			}

					has_trait = cynical

			}

				modifier = VIET_opinion_struggle_discussion_disagree

			}

VIETmisc.0259 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0259.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0259.desc
	theme = death
	override_background = { reference = throne_room }
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:teddy_roosevelt
		animation = personality_bold
	}

	trigger = {
		any_vassal = {
			is_available_healthy_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = brave
				has_trait = arrogant
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = calm
				has_trait = paranoid
				has_trait = content
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = craven
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_available_healthy_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = brave
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = calm
					has_trait = paranoid
					has_trait = content
				}
				NOT = {
					has_trait = craven
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = teddy_roosevelt
			VIET_huge_prestige_gain_effect = yes
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_teddy_roosevelt_assassination
				years = 10
			}
			increase_wounds_no_death_effect = { REASON = assassination }
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0259.a
		VIET_huge_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		scope:teddy_roosevelt = {
			VIET_huge_prestige_gain_effect = yes
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_praised_me
			}			
		}
		stress_impact = {
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			sadistic = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0259.b
	}

}

VIETmisc.0265 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0265.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0265.desc
	theme = friendly
	override_background = { reference = throne_room }
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:prude
		animation = disapproval
	}
	
	right_portrait = { 	
		character = scope:musician
		animation = personality_compassionate
	}

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = lifestyle_poet
				diplomacy >= decent_skill_rating
				learning >= decent_skill_rating
			}
			NOR = {
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = chaste
				has_trait = wrathful
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				has_trait = stubborn
				has_trait = chaste
				has_trait = wrathful
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = honest
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_poet
					diplomacy >= decent_skill_rating
					learning >= decent_skill_rating
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = chaste
					has_trait = wrathful
					has_trait = zealous
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = musician
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_vulgar_music
				years = 9
			}
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = stubborn
					has_trait = chaste
					has_trait = wrathful
					has_trait = zealous
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = prude
		}
		scope:prude = {			
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:musician
				modifier = VIET_opinion_composes_vulgar_music
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = scope:musician
				modifier = VIET_opinion_dislikes_my_music
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0265.a
		stress_impact = {
			chaste = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:prude = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_supports_vulgar_music
			}
		}
		scope:musician = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_supports_music
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0265.b
		stress_impact = {
			honest = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:musician = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_discreet_vulgar_music
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0265.c
		stress_impact = {
			shy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:prude = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root

			}
		}

		}

		}

		}
	}

				is_available_ai_adult = yes

				years = 10
			}
		}
	}

			just = medium_stress_impact_gain
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = minor_stress_impact_gain
			generous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			vengeful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_lenient_robber_baron
			years = 10

VIETmisc.0267 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0267.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0267.desc
	theme = vassal
	
	left_portrait = scope:vassal
	
	right_portrait = scope:official

	trigger = {
		any_vassal = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			is_landed = yes
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				is_landed = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = vassal
		}
		create_character = {
			employer = scope:vassal
			template = random_barrel_character
			gender_female_chance = root_faith_dominant_gender_female_chance
			faith = scope:vassal.faith
			culture = scope:vassal.culture
			save_scope_as = official
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:official = {
				random_list = {
					40 = { add_trait = shrewd }
					30 = { add_trait = intellect_good_1 }
					20 = { add_trait = intellect_good_2 }
					10 = { add_trait = intellect_good_3 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0267.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				gold >= 50
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = { 
				desc = VIETmisc.0267.a.toast.failure
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						opinion = {
							target = scope:vassal
							value > 0
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						opinion = {
							target = scope:vassal
							value > 33
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						opinion = {
							target = scope:vassal
							value > 66
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						has_trait = content
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						has_trait = lazy
					}
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					left_icon = scope:official
					title = VIETmisc.0267.a.toast.failure
					VIET_medium_prestige_loss_effect = yes
				}
			}
			50 = { 
				desc = VIETmisc.0267.a.toast.success
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						opinion = {
							target = scope:vassal
							value < 0
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 10
					scope:official = {
						opinion = {
							target = scope:vassal
							value < -33
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {

					}

					}

		}

		}
		scope:vassal = {

				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_helped_my_debt_problems
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
			paranoid = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = {

		}

		any_councillor = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOR = {
				has_trait = diligent
				has_trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_councillor = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOR = {
					has_trait = diligent
					has_trait = ambitious
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = councilor
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_tardy_to_meetings
				years = 10
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0272.a
		VIET_medium_prestige_loss_effect = yes
		scope:councilor = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_off_the_hook
			}
		}
		every_councillor = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { this = scope:councilor }
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_didnt_chastise_tardy_councilor
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			vengeful = minor_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = minor_stress_impact_gain
			paranoid = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			diligent = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0272.b
		scope:councilor = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_chastise_tardy
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			forgiving = minor_stress_impact_gain
			trusting = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			patient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		random = {
			chance = 30
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				left_icon = ROOT
				title = VIETmisc.0272.b.tooltip
				scope:councilor = {
					remove_character_modifier = VIET_tardy_to_meetings
				}
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
			}
		}
	}

						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
				}
				random_relation = {
					type = soulmate
					limit = {
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = lover
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_relation = {
						type = lover
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
				}
				random_relation = {
					type = lover
					limit = {
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = lover
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0294.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = lustful
			}
			stress_impact = {
				lustful = medium_stress_impact_gain
			}
		}
		else = {
			stress_impact = {
				base = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			}
		}
		scope:lover = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_snuggling
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_snuggling
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0294.b
		stress_impact = {
			chaste = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:lover = {
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_not_in_mood
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_unsatisfied_horny
			years = 3
		}
	}

}

	theme = romance_scheme

	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = shame
	}

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			any_vassal = {
				is_available_ai_child = yes
				root = { has_relation_crush = prev }
			}
			any_vassal = {
				any_child = {
					is_available_ai_child = yes
					root = { has_relation_crush = prev }
				}
			}
			any_courtier_or_guest = {
				is_available_ai_child = yes
				root = { has_relation_crush = prev }
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = pensive
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = charming
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		every_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_child = yes
				root = { has_relation_crush = prev }
			}
			add_to_list = crushes
		}
		every_vassal = {
			every_child = {
				limit = {
					is_available_ai_child = yes
					root = { has_relation_crush = prev }
				}
				add_to_list = crushes
			}
		}
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_child = yes
				root = { has_relation_crush = prev }
			}
			add_to_list = crushes
		}
				
		random_in_list = {
			list = crushes
			save_scope_as = crush
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0297.a
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_gain
			shy = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				desc = VIETmisc.0297.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					left_icon = ROOT
					title = VIETmisc.0297.a.failure
					scope:crush = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = root
							modifier = VIET_opinion_awkward
						}
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				desc = VIETmisc.0297.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					left_icon = ROOT
					title = VIETmisc.0297.a.success
					scope:crush = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = root
							modifier = amused_opinion
							opinion = 10
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

}

VIETmisc.0332 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0332.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0332.desc
	theme = friendly
	override_background = { reference = courtyard }

	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:courtier_1
		animation = personality_irrational
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:courtier_2
		animation = eyeroll
	}


	trigger = {	
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			count >= 2
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		every_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
			add_to_list = courtier_candidates
		}
		random_in_list = {
			list = courtier_candidates
			save_scope_as = courtier_1
		}
		random_in_list = {
			list = courtier_candidates
			limit = {
				exists = scope:courtier_1
				NOT = { this = scope:courtier_1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = courtier_2
		}
		scope:courtier_1 = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_embarrassing_misunderstanding
				years = 10
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:courtier_2
				modifier = humiliated_opinion
				opinion = -10
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0332.a
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_impact_loss
			compassionate = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:courtier_1 = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = humiliated_opinion
				opinion = -10
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0332.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_amused_ruler
			days = 365
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = { limit = {
				always = no
			} # Unreachable effects archived in debug-dead-branches/audit.json.
}

	}
	


}

				target = root

			}

				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_shirks_duties
			}

			}

			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -10
			}
		}
		scope:councilor_2 = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			base = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			sadistic = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
			content = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
		}
	}

VIETmisc.0343 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0343.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0343.desc
	theme = memory_positive
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:courtier
		animation = throne_room_conversation_2
	}
	right_portrait = root


	trigger = {	
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = VIET_snuggery_modifier }	
		OR = {
			any_courtier_or_guest = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				opinion = {
					target = root
					value >= 0
				}
			}
			any_vassal = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				opinion = {
					target = root
					value >= 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = trusting
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				opinion = {
					target = root
					value >= 0
				}
			}
			add_to_list = candidate_characters
		}
		every_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				opinion = {
					target = root
					value >= 0
				}
			}
			add_to_list = candidate_characters
		}
		random_in_list = {
			list = candidate_characters
			save_scope_as = courtier
		}
	}


	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0343.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				gold >= 50
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = 50
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_snuggery_modifier
			years = 20
		}
		scope:courtier = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_listened_to_my_advice
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_good_advice
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			diligent = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			greedy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0343.b
		stress_impact = {
			lazy = medium_stress_impact_gain
			shy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}

		}

					has_trait = education_learning_3
					has_trait = education_learning_4
				}
			}

					has_trait = lifestyle_scholar

					has_trait = education_learning_4
				}

		}

			add = 0.2

		}

		}

			add = -0.2

		}

					learning >= decent_skill_rating
					has_trait = lifestyle_scholar
					has_trait = theologian
					has_trait = education_learning_3
					has_trait = education_learning_4
				}
			}
			add_to_list = candidate_characters

			}

VIETmisc.0347 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0347.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0347.desc
	theme = learning_scholarship_focus
	override_background = { reference = throne_room }
	
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = thinking
	}

	trigger = {
		
		probably_unintelligent_trigger = no
		
		NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_tribal }
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0347.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_learning_math
			years = 3
		}
		stress_impact = {
			impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0347.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_indoors_nap
			years = 3
		}
		stress_impact = {
			diligent = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			ambitious = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}

	after = {		
		every_courtier = {
			custom = VIET_intellectual_courtiers
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				probably_unintelligent_trigger = no
				learning >= low_skill_rating
				OR = {
					has_trait = diligent
					has_trait = lifestyle_scholar
					has_trait = theologian
					has_trait = education_learning_1
					has_trait = education_learning_2
					has_trait = education_learning_3
					has_trait = education_learning_4
					learning >= medium_skill_rating
					stewardship >= medium_skill_rating
				}
				NOT = { has_trait = lazy }
				NOT = { has_trait = arbitrary }
				NOT = { has_trait = impatient }
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_learning_math
				years = 3
			}	
		}
		every_courtier = {
			custom = VIET_unintellectual_courtiers
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				probably_unintelligent_trigger = no
				learning <= medium_skill_rating
				OR = {
					has_trait = lazy
					has_trait = arbitrary
					has_trait = impatient
					learning <= low_skill_rating
					probably_unintelligent_trigger = yes
				}
				NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_scholar }
				NOT = { has_trait = theologian }
				NOT = { has_trait = education_learning_1 }
				NOT = { has_trait = education_learning_2 }
				NOT = { has_trait = education_learning_3 }
				NOT = { has_trait = education_learning_4 }
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_indoors_nap
				years = 3
			}		
		}
	}
	


}

VIETmisc.0351 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0351.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0351.desc
	theme = laamp
	override_background = { reference = bp1_bonfire }
	
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = marshal_axe
	}
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:superstitious
		animation = stayback
	}

	trigger = {	
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_travelling = no
				domicile ?= {
					domicile_location = {
						NOR = {
							terrain = drylands
							terrain = desert
							terrain = desert_mountains
							terrain = oasis
						}
					}
				}
				any_courtier = {
					is_available_ai_adult = yes
					NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
					OR = {
						has_trait = zealous
						has_trait = paranoid
						has_trait = eccentric
						has_trait = gregarious
						has_trait = craven
						has_trait = fickle
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_travelling = yes
				location = {
					NOR = {
						terrain = drylands
						terrain = desert
						terrain = desert_mountains
						terrain = oasis
					}					
				}
				current_travel_plan ?= {
					any_entourage_character = {	
						is_ai = yes
						is_available_travelling = yes
						NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
						OR = {
							has_trait = zealous
							has_trait = paranoid
							has_trait = eccentric
							has_trait = gregarious
							has_trait = craven
							has_trait = fickle
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = lazy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = honest
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 2
			domicile ?= {
				domicile_location = {
					OR = {
						terrain = forest
						terrain = jungle
						terrain = mountains
						terrain = hills
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_travelling = yes
				exists = current_travel_plan
			}
			current_travel_plan ?= {
				random_entourage_character = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
						is_available_travelling = yes
						NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
						OR = {
							has_trait = zealous
							has_trait = paranoid
							has_trait = eccentric
							has_trait = gregarious
							has_trait = craven
							has_trait = fickle
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = superstitious
				}	
			}	
			location.county = { save_scope_as = county }
			scope:county.culture = {
				save_scope_as = culture
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_available_ai_adult = yes
					NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
					OR = {
						has_trait = zealous
						has_trait = paranoid
						has_trait = eccentric
						has_trait = gregarious
						has_trait = craven
						has_trait = fickle
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = superstitious
			}
			root.domicile.domicile_location.county = {
				save_scope_as = county
			}
			scope:county.culture = {
				save_scope_as = culture
			}
		}

	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0351.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				gold >= 10
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = 10
		VIET_medium_piety_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			cynical = minor_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			greedy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0351.b
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				show_chance = no
				desc = VIETmisc.0351.b.nothing
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = VIETmisc.0351.b.nothing
					left_icon = root
					type = event_toast_effect_good
				}
			}
			100 = {
				show_chance = no
				desc = VIETmisc.0351.b.bad
				send_interface_toast = {
					title = VIETmisc.0351.b.bad
					left_icon = root
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					add_character_modifier = {
						modifier = VIET_forest_spirit_curse
						years = 3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			zealous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			humble = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			content = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	


}
