VIETmisc.0378 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0378.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0378.desc
	theme = friendly
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = frontend_right_idle
	}

	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:lover
		animation = laugh
	}
	
	trigger = {
		VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
		
		
		
		
		OR = {
			any_relation = {
				type = soulmate
				VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
			any_relation = {
				type = lover
				VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = lustful
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = chaste
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_relation = {
						type = soulmate
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
				}
				random_relation = {
					type = soulmate
					limit = {
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = lover
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_relation = {
						type = lover
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
				}
				random_relation = {
					type = lover
					limit = {
						VIET_sexual_character_trigger = yes
						is_available_ai_adult = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = lover
				}
			}
		}
		scope:lover = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_likes_lame_jokes
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}

	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0378.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_doesnt_like_lame_jokes
			years = 5
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			forgiving = minor_stress_impact_gain
			patient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			calm = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:lover = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_dislikes_my_lame_jokes
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_annoying_lame_jokes
			}
		}

	}	
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0378.b
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = VIET_likes_lame_jokes
			years = 5
		}
		stress_impact = {
			impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			wrathful = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:lover = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_likes_my_lame_jokes
			}
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_amused_by_lame_jokes
			}
		}

	}	


}

VIETmisc.0381 = {

		}

			add = -0.2

		}

VIETmisc.0384 = {
	type = character_event
	title = VIETmisc.0384.t
	desc = VIETmisc.0384.desc
	theme = learning_theology_focus
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { 
				OR = {
					has_trait = callous
					has_trait = sadistic
					has_trait = arrogant
					has_trait = eccentric
					has_trait = impatient
				}	
			}
			animation = eyeroll
		}
		animation = personality_forgiving
	}
	
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:chaplain
		animation = sadness
	}

	trigger = {	
		exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOT = { has_trait = arrogant }
		}
	}
	
	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = shy
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.2
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -0.2
			has_trait = sadistic
		}
	}	

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = liege
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			save_scope_as = chaplain
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_lacking_intellectual_self_esteem
				years = 10
			}
		}		
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0384.a
		stress_impact = {
			callous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			arrogant = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			cynical = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:chaplain = {
			remove_character_modifier = VIET_lacking_intellectual_self_esteem
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = VIET_encouraged_by_others
				years = 5
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = VIET_opinion_encouragement
			}					
		}
	}	
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0384.b
		stress_impact = {
			lazy = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
			cynical = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
		scope:chaplain = {
			duel = {
				skill = learning
				value = average_skill_rating
				10 = { 
					compare_modifier = {
						value = scope:duel_value
						multiplier = -0.8
						min = -49
					}
					desc = VIETmisc.0384.b.toast.failure
					scope:liege = {
						send_interface_toast = {
							type = event_toast_effect_bad
							left_icon = scope:chaplain
							title = VIETmisc.0384.b.toast.failure
							scope:chaplain = {	
								stress_impact = {
									base = medium_stress_impact_gain
								}
								add_opinion = {
									target = scope:liege
									modifier = frustrated_opinion
									opinion = -15
								}
							}	
						}
					}
				}
				10 = { 
					compare_modifier = {
						value = scope:duel_value
						multiplier = 0.8
						min = -49
					}
					desc = VIETmisc.0384.b.toast.success
					scope:liege = {
						send_interface_toast = {
							type = event_toast_effect_good
							left_icon = scope:chaplain
							title = VIETmisc.0384.b.toast.success
							scope:chaplain = {
								add_learning_skill = 1
								stress_impact = {
									base = medium_stress_impact_loss
								}
								add_character_modifier = {
									modifier = VIET_encouraged_by_others
									years = 10
								}
								add_opinion = {
									target = root
									modifier = VIET_opinion_encouragement
								}						
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}			
		}
	}	
	
	option = { 
		name = VIETmisc.0384.c
		VIET_huge_prestige_gain_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			zealous = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}	

}
